Tell Mia the name of the person she companions (MIADI_MIA_COMPANION_PERSON_NAME), and start phone-capture when it is not answering, so the iPhone can record into episodes on this host. phone-capture is silent on a host without MIADI_CHRONICLE_ROOT. ([jgwill/miadi-orchestration-kit](https://github.com/jgwill/miadi-orchestration-kit/blob/HEAD/claude/mia-episode-companion/hooks/hooks.json) · ★4)
- **miadi-chronicle-episode-kit**: PostToolUse, PreToolUse, Stop on Bash. Episode vessels are born by mkepisode, never mkdir. A PreToolUse hook on Bash refuses a command that would create a new directory directly under $MIADI_CHRONICLE_ROOT and returns the mkepisode invocation — with the --adopt repair for a directory that already holds work. Creating directories inside… ([jgwill/miadi-orchestration-kit](https://github.com/jgwill/miadi-orchestration-kit/blob/HEAD/claude/miadi-chronicle-episode-kit/hooks/hooks.json) · ★4)
- **miadi-deepdiver**: PreToolUse on Write|Edit. miadi-deepdiver: the word gate refuses a prompt or kept text that carries a word from forbidden-words.txt (first entry: the space between current reality and the desired outcome is written as resolve tension). Deterministic, no model. ([jgwill/miadi-orchestration-kit](https://github.com/jgwill/miadi-orchestration-kit/blob/HEAD/claude/miadi-deepdiver/hooks/hooks.json) · ★4)

- **miadi-session-issue**: SessionEnd, UserPromptSubmit. miadi-session-issue: a session that opts in gets one GitHub issue at its first prompt and a comment at each exit. A session that does not opt in is untouched. ([jgwill/miadi-orchestration-kit](https://github.com/jgwill/miadi-orchestration-kit/blob/HEAD/claude/miadi-session-issue/hooks/hooks.json) · ★4)
- **miadi-session-observability**: Notification, PermissionRequest, PostToolUse, PostToolUseFailure, PreCompact, PreToolUse, SessionEnd, SessionStart, Stop, SubagentStart, SubagentStop, UserPromptSubmit on Bash, Bash|Read|Grep, Write|Edit|MultiEdit|NotebookEdit|Bash. Miadi session capture: the 12 events and their commands, writing the &lt;root&gt;/&lt;session_id&gt;/ layout, plus the binding line (pane, command line, name history, team). - **mod**: UserPromptSubmit. Hybrid: the classic UserPromptSubmit hook runs everywhere; the module loads only where function hooks are on (Claude Code &gt;= 2.1.287, or CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_FUNCTION_HOOKS=1 from 2.1.277). While the module runs it sets MY_MOD_ACTIVE, and the classic hook does nothing, so no event fires twice.
